to certain limited exceptions, the initial shareholders have agreed not to transfer, assign, or sell their founder shares until six months
after the date of the consummation of the Company&#8217;s initial business combination or earlier if, subsequent to the initial business
combination, the Company consummates a subsequent liquidation, merger, share exchange, or other similar transaction that results in all
of the shareholders having the right to exchange their ordinary shares for cash, securities, or other property.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Extensions
Loan &#8211; Related Party</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Pursuant
to the amended and restated memorandum and articles of association of the Company then in effect, if the Company anticipates that it
may not be able to consummate a business combination within 12 months of the closing of the IPO, the Company may extend the period of
time to consummate a business combination up to twelve times by an additional one month each time to complete a business combination.
Pursuant to the terms of the Company&#8217;s memorandum and articles of association and the trust agreement entered into between the
Company and the Trustee, both as amended, in order to extend the time available for the Company to consummate a business combination,
the Sponsor its affiliates or designees, upon five days advance notice prior to the applicable deadline, must deposit into the Trust
Account the applicable extension fees, on or prior to the date of the applicable deadline, for each extension. The Sponsor or its affiliates
or designees will receive a non-interest bearing, unsecured promissory note equal to the amount of any such deposit that will not be
repaid in the event that the Company is unable to close a business combination unless there are funds available outside the Trust Account
to do so. Such notes would either be paid upon consummation of the Company&#8217;s initial business combination or at the lender&#8217;s
discretion, converted upon consummation of the business combination into additional private units at a price of $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1_iI_c20251231_zdJyJiqP6rJ2" title="Conversion price per unit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31" id="Fact000600" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per unit.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">During
the years ended December 31, 2025 and 2024, the Company entered into monthly extension loans with its Sponsor for $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrust_iI_c20251231__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--MonthlyExtensionLoanMember_zfIiTYB0Q93c" title="Deposit into the Trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrust" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_SponsorMember_custom_MonthlyExtensionLoanMember" id="Fact000602" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,012,031</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrust_iI_c20241231__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--MonthlyExtensionLoanMember_z1fNKgOji4Y4" title="Deposit into the Trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrust" contextRef="AsOf2024-12-31_custom_SponsorMember_custom_MonthlyExtensionLoanMember" id="Fact000604" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">113,850</ix:nonFraction></span>,
respectively, and United Hydrogen for $<span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrust_iI_c20251231__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--UnitedHydrogenMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--MonthlyExtensionLoanMember_zrLcmkdeUZY1" title="Deposit into the Trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrust" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_UnitedHydrogenMember_custom_MonthlyExtensionLoanMember" id="Fact000606" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,012,031</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrust_iI_c20241231__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--UnitedHydrogenMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--MonthlyExtensionLoanMember_zTkoTandXelg" title="Deposit into the Trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrust" contextRef="AsOf2024-12-31_custom_UnitedHydrogenMember_custom_MonthlyExtensionLoanMember" id="Fact000608" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">113,850</ix:nonFraction></span>, respectively; these funds were deposited into the Trust Account in order
to extend the time available to complete a business combination. These loans are non-interest-bearing, payable upon the closing of a
business combination, and convertible, at the lender&#8217;s discretion, upon consummation of the business combination, into additional
private units at a price of $<span id="xdx_905_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1_iI_c20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorAndUnitedHydrogenMember_z9fI4F4T3Nk2" title="Conversion price per unit"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember_custom_SponsorAndUnitedHydrogenMember" id="Fact000610" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per unit. As of December 31, 2025 and 2024, the extension loan balances were $<span id="xdx_904_eus-gaap--NotesPayable_iI_c20251231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorAndUnitedHydrogenMember_ztZS8Dbb5n66" title="Notes payable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesPayable" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember_custom_SponsorAndUnitedHydrogenMember" id="Fact000612" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">2,024,062</ix:nonFraction></span> and $<span id="xdx_90C_eus-gaap--NotesPayable_iI_c20241231__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorAndUnitedHydrogenMember_zcRusX4EVfPj" title="Notes payable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NotesPayable" contextRef="AsOf2024-12-31_custom_UnsecuredPromissoryNoteMember_custom_SponsorAndUnitedHydrogenMember" id="Fact000614" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">227,700</ix:nonFraction></span>,
respectively.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Working
Capital Loans</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
order to finance transaction costs in connection with a business combination, the Company&#8217;s Sponsor or an affiliate of the Sponsor,
or the Company&#8217;s officers and directors may, but are not obligated to, loan the Company funds as may be required (&#8220;Working
Capital Loans&#8221;). Such Working Capital Loans would be evidenced by promissory notes. The notes would either be repaid upon consummation
of a business combination, without interest, or, at the lender&#8217;s discretion, up to $<span id="xdx_90D_eus-gaap--StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueConversionOfUnits_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember__srt--RangeAxis__srt--MaximumMember_zEj1OvXhOry" title="Stock issued during period, value, conversion of units"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:StockIssuedDuringPeriodValueConversionOfUnits" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_custom_SponsorMember_srt_MaximumMember" id="Fact000616" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,500,000</ix:nonFraction></span> of notes may be converted upon consummation
of a business combination into additional Private Units at a price of $<span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1_iI_pid_c20251231__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember_zy7kX8BHfuUg" title="Debt conversion price per share"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_SponsorMember" id="Fact000618" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span> per Unit. In the event that a business combination does
not close, the Company may use a portion of proceeds held outside the Trust Account to repay the Working Capital Loans, but no proceeds
held in the Trust Account would be used to repay the Working Capital Loans. As of December 31, 2025 and 2024, there was <span id="xdx_901_eus-gaap--LoansPayable_iI_do_c20251231__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember_zwDb7c2a5Cu3" title="Working capital loan payable"><span id="xdx_909_eus-gaap--LoansPayable_iI_do_c20241231__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember_zx2yMlORJZD5" title="Working capital loan payable"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LoansPayable" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_custom_SponsorMember" id="Fact000620" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:LoansPayable" contextRef="AsOf2024-12-31_custom_SponsorMember" id="Fact000622" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">no</ix:nonFraction></ix:nonFraction></span></span> amount outstanding
under any Working Capital Loan.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Rights</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Each
holder of a right will receive one-fifth (1/5) of one ordinary share upon consummation of a business combination, even if the holder
of such right redeemed all shares held by it in connection with a business combination. No fractional shares will be issued upon exchange
of the rights. No additional consideration will be required to be paid by a holder of rights in order to receive its additional shares
upon consummation of a business combination, as the consideration related thereto has been included in the unit purchase price paid for
by investors in the Initial Public Offering. If the Company enters into a definitive agreement for a business combination in which the
Company will not be the surviving entity, the definitive agreement will provide for the holders of rights to receive the same per share
consideration the holders of the ordinary shares will receive in the transaction on an as-converted into ordinary share basis, and each
holder of a right will be required to affirmatively convert its rights in order to receive one-fifth (1/5) of one share underlying each
right (without paying additional consideration). The shares issuable upon exchange of the rights will be freely tradable (except to the
extent held by affiliates of the Company).</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Additionally,
in no event will the Company be required to net cash to settle the rights. If the Company is unable to complete a business combination
within the Combination Period and the Company liquidates the funds held in the Trust Account, holders of rights will not receive any
of such funds with respect to their rights, nor will they receive any distribution from the Company&#8217;s assets held outside of the
Trust Account with respect to such rights. Accordingly, the rights may expire worthless.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-01-01to2025-12-31" escape="true" id="Fact000658" name="us-gaap:CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_806_eus-gaap--CommitmentsAndContingenciesDisclosureTextBlock_zwnghCarXTW3" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 26pt; text-align: justify; text-indent: -26pt"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
7 &#8211; <span id="xdx_82D_zv6oLQsJhn4l">COMMITMENTS AND CONTINGENCIES</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Registration
Rights</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
holders of the insider shares, as well as the holders of the Private Units (and underlying securities) and any securities issued in payment
of Working Capital Loans made to the Company, will be entitled to registration rights pursuant to an agreement to be signed prior to
or on the effective date of the Initial Public Offering. The holders of a majority of these securities are entitled to make up to three
demands that the Company register such securities at any time after the Company consummates a business combination. In addition, the
holders have certain &#8220;piggy-back&#8221; registration rights with respect to registration statements filed subsequent to the consummation
of a business combination. The Company will bear the expenses incurred in connection with the filing of any such registration statements.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
initial shareholders and their permitted transferees can demand that the Company register the founder shares, the Private Units and the
underlying Private Shares, and the units issuable upon conversion of working capital loans and the underlying ordinary shares and rights,
pursuant to an agreement to be signed prior to or on the effective date requiring the Company to register such securities for resale.
The holders of such securities are entitled to demand that the Company register these securities at any time after consummation of an
initial business combination. Notwithstanding anything to the contrary, any holder that is affiliated with an underwriter participating
in the Initial Public Offering may only make a demand on one occasion and only during the five-year period beginning on the effective
date of the registration statement. In addition, the holders have certain &#8220;piggy-back&#8221; registration rights on registration
statements filed after the Company&#8217;s consummation of a business combination; provided that any holder that is affiliated with an
underwriter participating in the Initial Public Offering may participate in a &#8220;piggy-back&#8221; registration only during the seven-year
period beginning on the effective date of the registration statement.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Representative
Shares</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company issued <span id="xdx_902_eus-gaap--SharesIssued_iI_c20251231__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zxpX4tvqtLM2" title="Issuance of representative shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssued" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000660" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">69,000</ix:nonFraction></span> ordinary shares to the representative (and/or its designees) (the &#8220;representative shares&#8221;) as part
of representative compensation as the underwriters exercised their over-allotment option in full. The representative shares have been
deemed compensation by FINRA and are therefore subject to a lock-up for a period of 180 days immediately following the date of the commencement
of sales in the Initial Public Offering pursuant to FINRA Rule 5110(e)(1). Pursuant to FINRA Rule 5110(e)(1), these securities will not
be the subject of any hedging, short sale, derivative, put, or call transaction that would result in the economic disposition of the
securities by any person for a period of 180 days immediately following the date of the commencement of sales in the Initial Public Offering,
nor may they be sold, transferred, assigned, pledged, or hypothecated for a period of 180 days immediately following the date of the
commencement of sales in the Initial Public Offering except to any underwriter and selected dealer participating in the offering and
their officers, partners, registered persons, or affiliates.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"></span></p>

<ix:exclude><!-- Field: Page; Sequence: 60; Value: 1 -->
    <div style="border-bottom: Black 1pt solid; margin-bottom: 6pt"><table cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" style="border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; font-size: 10pt"><tr style="vertical-align: top; text-align: left"><td style="text-align: center; width: 100%">F-<!-- Field: Sequence; Type: Arabic; Name: PageNo -->16<!-- Field: /Sequence --></td></tr></table></div>
    <div style="break-before: page; margin-top: 6pt"><p style="margin: 0pt">&#160;</p></div>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Underwriter
Agreement</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
underwriters purchased <span id="xdx_90F_eus-gaap--SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zLxB2QiT6SNh" title="Number of units issued"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SaleOfStockNumberOfSharesIssuedInTransaction" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000662" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">900,000</ix:nonFraction></span> additional Units to cover over-allotments.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
underwriters were entitled to a cash underwriting discount of two percent (<span id="xdx_903_ecustom--UnderwritingDiscountOnInitialPublicOfferingPercentage_pid_dp_uPure_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zFnsTYRiGbIk" title="Underwriting discount percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="AFJK:UnderwritingDiscountOnInitialPublicOfferingPercentage" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000664" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">2.00</ix:nonFraction></span>%) of the gross proceeds of the Initial Public Offering,
or $<span id="xdx_908_ecustom--CashUnderwritingDiscount_iI_c20251231__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zLPEwRR4btCj" title="Cash underwriting discount"><ix:nonFraction name="AFJK:CashUnderwritingDiscount" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000666" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">1,380,000</ix:nonFraction></span>, as the underwriters&#8217; over-allotment was exercised in full. In addition, the underwriters are entitled to a deferred
fee of one percent (<span id="xdx_906_ecustom--DeferredFeeOnInitialPublicOfferingPercentage_pid_dp_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zJhiX8EvlMVl" title="Deferred fee percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="AFJK:DeferredFeeOnInitialPublicOfferingPercentage" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000668" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">1.0</ix:nonFraction></span>%) of the gross proceeds of the Initial Public Offering, or $<span id="xdx_903_ecustom--DeferredUnderwritingFeePayable_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--IPOMember_zPGzU9z7VME2" title="Deferred underwriting fee payable"><ix:nonFraction name="AFJK:DeferredUnderwritingFeePayable" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_us-gaap_IPOMember" id="Fact000670" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">690,000</ix:nonFraction></span>, as the underwriters&#8217; over-allotment
was exercised in full, upon closing of the business combination. The deferred fee will be paid in cash upon the closing of a business
combination from the amounts held in the Trust Account, subject to the terms of the underwriting agreement. In addition, the Company
paid the representative of the underwriters, at the closing of the Initial Public Offering, <span id="xdx_908_ecustom--DeferredFeeOnInitialPublicOfferingPercentage_pid_dp_c20250101__20251231__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_z7Rkw4Y631N7" title="Deferred fee percentage"><ix:nonFraction name="AFJK:DeferredFeeOnInitialPublicOfferingPercentage" contextRef="From2025-01-012025-12-31_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000672" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" scale="-2" unitRef="Pure">1.00</ix:nonFraction></span>% of the gross proceeds in the Company&#8217;s
ordinary shares, or <span id="xdx_907_eus-gaap--SharesIssued_iI_c20251231__us-gaap--SubsidiarySaleOfStockAxis__us-gaap--OverAllotmentOptionMember_zqcppdwkOvXl" title="Issuance of representative shares"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:SharesIssued" contextRef="AsOf2025-12-31_us-gaap_OverAllotmentOptionMember" id="Fact000674" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="Shares">69,000</ix:nonFraction></span> ordinary shares, as the underwriters&#8217; over-allotment was exercised in full.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b><i>Right
of First Refusal</i></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">For
a period beginning on the closing of the Initial Public Offering and ending 12 months from the closing of a business combination, the
Company has granted Spartan Capital Securities, LLC, a right of first refusal to act as the sole investment banker, sole book running
manager and/or sole placement agent for any and all future private or public equity, equity-linked, convertible and debt offerings during
such period. In accordance with FINRA Rule 5110(g)(6)(A), such right of first refusal shall not have a duration of more than three years
from the commencement of sales in the Initial Public Offering.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-01-01to2025-12-31" escape="true" id="Fact000676" name="us-gaap:SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock"><p id="xdx_801_eus-gaap--SegmentReportingDisclosureTextBlock_zhok2TxSzCq6" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
8 &#8211; <span id="xdx_829_z9hydfmQUmo2">SEGMENT INFORMATION</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">ASC
Topic 280, &#8220;Segment Reporting,&#8221; establishes standards for companies to report in their financial statement information about
operating segments, products, services, geographic areas, and major customers. Operating segments are defined as components of an enterprise
for which separate financial information is available that is regularly evaluated by the Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker,
or group, in deciding how to allocate resources and assess performance.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
Company&#8217;s chief operating decision maker has been identified as the Chief Financial Officer (&#8220;CODM&#8221;), who reviews the
operating results for the Company as a whole to make decisions about allocating resources and assessing financial performance. Accordingly,
management has determined that the Company only has <span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--NumberOfOperatingSegments_pid_dc_uSegment_c20250101__20251231_z2r9gdqwRRM3" title="Number of operating segment"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:NumberOfOperatingSegments" contextRef="From2025-01-01to2025-12-31" id="Fact000678" format="ixt-sec:numwordsen" decimals="INF" unitRef="Segment">one</ix:nonFraction></span> operating segment.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">When
evaluating the Company&#8217;s performance and making key decisions regarding resource allocation, the CODM reviews several key metrics,
which include general and administrative expenses and interest earned on cash held in the Trust Account which are included in the accompanying
statements of operations.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">The
key measures of segment profit or loss reviewed by our CODM are interest earned on cash held in the Trust Account and general and
administrative expenses. The CODM reviews interest earned on cash held in the Trust Account to measure and monitor
stockholder value and determine the most effective strategy of investment with the Trust Account funds while maintaining compliance
with the trust agreement. General and administrative expenses are reviewed and monitored by the CODM to manage and forecast cash to
ensure enough capital is available to complete a business combination within the business combination period. The CODM also reviews
general and administrative costs to manage, maintain, and enforce all contractual agreements to ensure costs are aligned with all
agreements and budget.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><ix:nonNumeric contextRef="From2025-01-01to2025-12-31" escape="true" id="Fact000680" name="us-gaap:SubsequentEventsTextBlock"><p id="xdx_803_eus-gaap--SubsequentEventsTextBlock_zJYhgK6CAR04" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 26pt; text-align: justify; text-indent: -26pt"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt"><b>NOTE
9 &#8211; <span id="xdx_825_z41KtwGyYTee">SUBSEQUENT EVENTS</span></b></span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">In
accordance with ASC Topic 855, &#8220;<i>Subsequent Events</i>&#8221;, which establishes general standards of accounting for and disclosure
of events that occur after the balance sheet date, the Company has evaluated all events or transactions that occurred after the balance
sheet date through the date the financial statements were issued.</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>

<p style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: justify"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">Subsequent
to December 31, 2025, the Company entered into monthly extension loans with its Sponsor for $<span id="xdx_903_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrust_iI_c20260101__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--MonthlyExtensionLoanMember__us-gaap--SubsequentEventTypeAxis__us-gaap--SubsequentEventMember_zIrtzWhcpsq" title="Deposit into the Trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrust" contextRef="AsOf2026-01-01_custom_SponsorMember_custom_MonthlyExtensionLoanMember_us-gaap_SubsequentEventMember" id="Fact000682" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">17,165</ix:nonFraction></span>
and United Hydrogen for $<span id="xdx_906_eus-gaap--AssetsHeldInTrust_iI_c20260101__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--UnitedHydrogenMember__us-gaap--DebtInstrumentAxis__custom--MonthlyExtensionLoanMember__us-gaap--SubsequentEventTypeAxis__us-gaap--SubsequentEventMember_z8pomrO4oVEc" title="Deposit into the Trust Account"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:AssetsHeldInTrust" contextRef="AsOf2026-01-01_custom_UnitedHydrogenMember_custom_MonthlyExtensionLoanMember_us-gaap_SubsequentEventMember" id="Fact000684" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="0" unitRef="USD">17,165</ix:nonFraction> each month</span>,
which are deposited into the Trust Account in order to extend the time available to complete a business combination through January
5, 2026, February 12, 2026, March 4, 2026, April 7, 2026 and May 7, 2026. These loans are non-interest-bearing, payable upon the closing of a
business combination, and convertible, at the lender&#8217;s discretion, upon consummation of the business combination, into
additional private units at a price of $<span id="xdx_900_eus-gaap--DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1_iI_pid_c20260101__us-gaap--RelatedPartyTransactionsByRelatedPartyAxis__custom--SponsorMember__us-gaap--SubsequentEventTypeAxis__us-gaap--SubsequentEventMember_zOjXsFwrri74" title="Debt conversion price per share"><ix:nonFraction name="us-gaap:DebtInstrumentConvertibleConversionPrice1" contextRef="AsOf2026-01-01_custom_SponsorMember_us-gaap_SubsequentEventMember" id="Fact000686" format="ixt:numdotdecimal" decimals="INF" unitRef="USDPShares">10.00</ix:nonFraction></span>
per unit.</span></p>

</ix:nonNumeric><p id="xdx_81C_zvlOUIBvalT4" style="font: 10pt Times New Roman, Times, Serif; margin: 0pt 0pt 0pt 0; text-align: center"><span style="font-family: Times New Roman, Times, Serif; font-size: 10pt">&#160;</span></p>
